```
// 原版每个 GenPass 执行前 Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed)
// （1456 WorldGenerator.cs:497；服务器 exe IL RunPass：newobj UnifiedRandom::
// ctor(Int32) + stsfld Main::rand），GenBase._random => genRand => Main.rand
// ⇒ 每个 pass 的掷骰流各自从 stream(seed) 第 0 掷开始，pass 间【不续流】。
// terrain 段下方 :199 的重绑即此语义；本改造把它推广到链内全部 44 个 pass 边界
// （对照 AddGenerationPass 注册序 WorldGen.cs:11540-15887）。pass 内多检查点段
// （Desert 的 mound/entrance/hive、Dungeon 的 17 段、Shimmer 的 shell/pillars/
// open）是【一个 pass 的内部段】，只在 pass 头重播一次。
Type URType = asm.GetType("Terraria.Utilities.UnifiedRandom")!;
void Replay() => r = (dynamic)Activator.CreateInstance(URType, seed)!;


// ---- 网格（vanilla tile id；行主序 y*W+x，同 JS TileStore）----
var act = new byte[W * H];
var typ = new ushort[W * H];
var wal = new ushort[W * H];
var liq = new byte[W * H];
var liqT = new byte[W * H];
var checkpoints = new List<string>();
var jsegPts = new List<string>();   // jungle 段级流指纹（对拍原版 JungleProbe）

static byte[] ToBytes(ushort[] a) { var b = new byte[a.Length * 2]; Buffer.BlockCopy(a, 0, b, 0, b.Length); return b; }
uint Fnv16(ushort[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ (uint)(v & 0xff)) * 16777619u; h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 8) & 0xff)) * 16777619u; } return h; }
static uint Fnv8(byte[] a) { uint h = 0x811c9dc5; foreach (var v in a) { h = (h ^ v) * 16777619u; h = h * 16777619u; } return h; }
// 只比活性 type（非活性格的幽灵 type 归零——与 JS 侧 hashGrids 口径一致；
// 幽灵 parity 是独立对账项：原版幽灵会存盘、我们 finalize 清除，玩法无差）
var hashTyp = new ushort[W * H];
var hashGho = new ushort[W * H];
var ghostPts = new List<string>();
void Checkpoint(string name)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < typ.Length; i++) {
        hashTyp[i] = (ushort)(act[i] != 0 ? typ[i] : 0);
        hashGho[i] = (ushort)(act[i] == 0 ? typ[i] : 0); // inactive 格残留 type（幽灵 parity）
    }
    checkpoints.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashTyp):x8} {Fnv8(act):x8} {Fnv16(wal):x8} {Fnv8(liq):x8}");
    ghostPts.Add($"{name}|{Fnv16(hashGho):x8}");
    // GRID_DUMP=pyramids:/tmp/cs-pyr → 命中检查点时落 typ(LE16)/act/wal(LE16)/liq 裸数组（对拍用）
    var gd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("GRID_DUMP");
    if (gd != null && gd.Split(':')[0] == name)
    {
        string p = gd.Split(':')[1];
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".typ", ToBytes(typ));
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".act", act);
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".wal", ToBytes(wal));
        File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".liq", liq);
        Console.WriteLine($"griddump {name} -> {p}");
    }
    // STATE_DUMP=dirtwallcleanup:/tmp/cs-st39 → 同上 + UnifiedRandom 内部态（结构段状态恢复对拍：
    // 上游 pass 多代理并发修改期，从该态直跑 #40-44 裁决，等价全链且不受上游 WIP 影响）
    var sd = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("STATE_DUMP");
    if (sd != null && sd.Split(':')[0] == name)
    {
        string p2 = sd.Split(':')[1];
        using var fs = File.Create(p2);
        using var bw = new BinaryWriter(fs);
        var urt = asm.GetType("Terraria.Utilities.UnifiedRandom")!;
        var usa = (int[])urt.GetField("SeedArray", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
        var uix = (uint)urt.GetField("inext", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)!.GetValue(r)!;
        bw.Write(W); bw.Write(H); bw.Write(uix);
        for (int q = 0; q < 56; q++) bw.Write(usa[q]);
        bw.Write(act, 0, act.Length);
        bw.Write(ToBytes(typ), 0, W * H * 2);
        bw.Write(ToBytes(wal), 0, W * H * 2);
        bw.Write(liq, 0, liq.Length);
        bw.Write(liqT, 0, liqT.Length);
        Console.WriteLine($"statedump {name} -> {p2}");
    }
}
```

```
355	/** 种子等价对账钩子（terrain oracle）：只跑 Reset 掷骰 + Terrain pass，
356	 *  返回 GenState 与逐列地表（trunc(num3)），供与 C# 参考实现逐列比对 */
357	export function runResetAndTerrain(cfg: { width: number; height: number; seedText: string; evil?: -1 | 0 | 1 }): {
358	  gs: GenState; surface: Int32Array; seed: number; world: World; rng: RNG; ctx: GenCtx;
359	} {
360	  const seed = translateSeed(cfg.seedText);
361	  const world = new World(cfg.width, cfg.height, seed);
362	  world.seedFlags = parseSeed(cfg.seedText).flags;
363	  const rng = new RNG(seed);
364	  const ctx: GenCtx = {
365	    world, store: world.store, rng,
366	    surface: new Float32Array(cfg.width),
367	    cfg: { ...cfg, evil: cfg.evil ?? -1 },
368	  };
369	  vanillaTerrain(ctx);
370	  const out = new Int32Array(cfg.width);
371	  for (let x = 0; x < cfg.width; x++) out[x] = ctx.surface[x];
372	  // rng 必须回传重播后的活对象（terrain 前已重绑 ctx.rng，链式对账续用同一条流）
373	  return { gs: ctxGs[0]!, surface: out, seed, world, rng: ctx.rng, ctx };
374	}

376	function vanillaTerrain(ctx: GenCtx) {
377	  const { store, world, surface, cfg } = ctx;

382	  gs.seed = translateSeed(cfg.seedText);
383	  // 彩蛋种子旗标（SeedEasterEggs.parseSeed）——特殊种子 pass 分支消费（GemPasses 等）；
384	  // 金标种子 flags 恒空集 → 掷骰流位置不受影响
385	  gs.seedFlags = parseSeed(cfg.seedText).flags;

387	  // 原版在 AddPasses 循环之前按此固定顺序消耗 Main.rand（种子等价的流位置契约）。
388	  // 天气/背景/月亮等值我们未消费——照掷照弃，只保留 GenState 下游字段。
389	  const W = store.w;

391	  //    JungleShrinePass 复用本值（曾照掷照弃致 pass 内重掷、流位+样式双偏）
392	  gs.jungleHut = rng.nextInt(5);
393	  // ② crimsonLeft（cs:11235）——邪恶群系左右分布，未消费

399	    rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);             // Next(2)（符号）
400	    if (wf !== 0) break;                // ×0.35×(±1) 非零 ⇔ wf≠0
401	  }
402	  // ④ 地狱箱主件洗牌（cs:11262-11274）：Next(5..1) 逐个抽出 → gs.hellChestItems
403	  {
404	    const pool = [274, 220, 112, 218, 3019];
405	    const out: number[] = [];
406	    while (pool.length > 0) out.push(...pool.splice(rng.nextInt(pool.length), 1));
407	    gs.hellChestItems = out;
408	  }
409	  // ⑤⑥ slimeRainTime / cloudBGActive（cs:11277-11283，天气初值，未消费）

411	  rng.nextIntRange(8640, 86400);
412	  // ⑦-⑩ 矿石替代对（cs:11286-11298）——Next(2)==0 用替代矿
413	  gs.oreTiers = {

415	    iron: rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_lead']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_iron']!,
416	    silver: rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tungsten']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_silver']!,
417	    gold: rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_platinum']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_gold']!,
418	  };
419	  // ⑪ crimson（cs:11321）+ UI 覆盖（骰照掷仅覆盖结果，cs:11326-11332）

422	  else if (cfg.evil === 1) gs.crimson = true;
423	  world.crimson = gs.crimson;

429	  gs.treeStyle = ts.treeStyle;

431	  rng.nextIntRange(1050, 3150);                            // caveBackX[0]
432	  {
433	    const a = rng.nextInt(8);
434	    let b = rng.nextInt(8);

439	  rng.nextInt(4);

441	  rng.nextInt(2);

443	  {
444	    const rollForest = () => {
445	      let n = rng.nextInt(14);
446	      if ((n === 1 || n === 2) && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) n = rng.nextInt(14);
447	      if (n === 0) n = rng.nextInt(14);
448	      if (n === 3 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 3) === 0) n = 31;
449	      if (n === 5 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) n = 51;
450	      if (n === 7 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 4) === 0) n = rng.nextIntRange(71, 74);
451	      return n;
452	    };
453	    const bg1 = rollForest();
454	    let bg2 = rollForest();
455	    while (bg2 === bg1) bg2 = rollForest();
456	    let bg3 = rollForest();
457	    while (bg3 === bg1 || bg3 === bg2) bg3 = rollForest();
458	    let bg4 = rollForest();
459	    while (bg4 === bg1 || bg4 === bg2 || bg4 === bg3) bg4 = rollForest();
460	    { // Corruption 背景：Next(6)；==5 时 Next(2) 定 51/52

471	    if (snowBG === 4 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);
472	    rng.nextInt(6);                                        // setBG(4, hallow)
473	    rng.nextInt(7);                                        // setBG(5, crimson)
474	    { // Desert 背景：Next(6)；==5 时 Next(5)

477	    }
478	    rng.nextInt(8);                                        // setBG(7, ice)
479	    rng.nextInt(5);                                        // setBG(8, mushroom)
480	    rng.nextInt(3);                                        // setBG(9, underworld)
481	  }
482	  // ⑯ RandomizeMoonState（cs:7748）——Next(9)，未消费

492	  }

494	  // ⑲ 地牢侧锚点 num7（cs:11394-11416）：Next(w) 拒绝采样到地牢侧 15% 区间
495	  let dX = rng.nextInt(W);
496	  const dLo = W * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.60 : 0.25);
497	  const dHi = W * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.75 : 0.40);
498	  while (dX < dLo || dX > dHi) dX = rng.nextInt(W);
```

```
413	    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();

415	    fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1]]);

419	    treeStyle[0] = roll6(); treeStyle[1] = roll6(); treeStyle[2] = roll6();
420	    while (treeStyle[1] === treeStyle[0]) treeStyle[1] = roll6();
421	    while (treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[0] || treeStyle[2] === treeStyle[1]) treeStyle[2] = roll6();
422	    treeX[2] = w;
423	    fix0([treeStyle[0], treeStyle[1], treeStyle[2]]);
424	  } else {
```

```
23	      return;

36	                [f.rgba.buffer],
37	              );
38	            }
39	            : undefined,
40	        },
41	        (label, p) => ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'progress', phase: 'generate', label, p }),

45	      const { packet, transfer } = packWorld(world); // 转移所有权（本分支最后一步）

47	      return;
48	    }

52	        ? req.save

54	      if (!data) throw new Error('存档数据缺失');
55	      ctx.postMessage({ id: req.id, type: 'progress', phase: 'parse', p: 0.3 });

61	      await settleWorldLiquids(world, 'load',
```

```
198	    const content = [
199	      ['UI_WorldCreation_PreviewSizeSmall', 'UI_WorldCreation_PreviewSizeMedium', 'UI_WorldCreation_PreviewSizeLarge'][this.sel.size],

203	      ['UI_WorldCreation_PreviewEvilRandom', 'UI_WorldCreation_PreviewEvilCorruption', 'UI_WorldCreation_PreviewEvilCrimson'][this.sel.evil],
204	    ];
205	    const border = 'UI_WorldCreation_PreviewBorder';
206	    // 贴图缓存 + 全层重画（onload 乱序到达也不串层）
207	    const draw = () => {
208	      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 84, 84);
209	      for (const key of content) {
210	        const im = this.previewImgs.get(key);
211	        const ime = im as unknown as HTMLImageElement | undefined;
212	        if (ime?.complete && ime.width) ctx.drawImage(ime, 4, 4);
213	      }
214	      const b = this.previewImgs.get(border);
215	      const be = b as unknown as HTMLImageElement | undefined;
216	      if (be?.complete && be.width) ctx.drawImage(be, 0, 0);
217	    };
218	    for (const key of [...content, border]) {
219	      if (this.previewImgs.has(key)) continue;
220	      const im = new Image();
221	      im.onload = () => draw();
222	      im.src = `sprites/vanilla-ui/${key}.png`;
223	      this.previewImgs.set(key, im);
224	    }
225	    draw();
226	  }

232	      : flags.drunkWorld ? 'Seed_Drunk'
233	      : flags.notTheBees ? 'Seed_NotTheBees'
234	      : flags.getGoodWorld ? 'Seed_ForTheWorthy'
235	      : flags.theConstant ? 'Seed_TheConstant'
236	      : flags.skyblock ? 'Seed_Skyblock'
237	      : flags.tenthAnniversary ? 'Seed_Celebration'
238	      : flags.noTraps ? 'Seed_NoTraps'
239	      : flags.remix ? 'Seed_Remix'
240	      : 'Seed_Normal';
241	    (this.seedIcon as HTMLImageElement).src = `sprites/vanilla-ui/UI_WorldCreation_${key}.png`;
242	  }

248	      || parseSeed(seed).specialName || Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.WorldCreation.DefaultName');
249	    this.close(() => this.flow.onCreate({ name, seed, w: size.w, h: size.h, difficulty: this.sel.diff, evil: EVILS[this.sel.evil].value }));
250	  }
```

```
import { BestiaryTracker, type BestiarySave } from '../data/Bestiary';
import { createJourneyPowers, type JourneyPowersState } from './JourneyPowers';
import { mainDifficulty, GDL } from '../stats/ScaleStats';

export interface ChestData { x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number } | null>; locked?: boolean; }

/** 死亡掉钱保留比例（返回值 = 留在身上的份额；Player.DropCoins :53351-53390）：
 *  经典/旅程世界掉一半 / 专家掉 3/4 / 大师全掉——按**世界难度** Main.GameMode
 *  （0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程按经典，Main.cs:2775-2780）。角色难度只决定
 *  是否走 DropCoins（见 Appearance.dropsCoinsOnDeath），不决定档位 */
export function deathCoinKeepFraction(worldDifficulty: number): number {
  // 原版 Player.DropCoins :53351-53380：num2 = stack/2(经典)/stack/4(专家)/0(大师) 是
  // **留在身上的份额**（num3 = stack - num2 才落地）——曾把 num2 误当掉出份额写反。
  if (worldDifficulty === 2) return 0;      // masterMode：num2=0 全掉（:53359）
  if (worldDifficulty === 1) return 0.25;   // expertMode：num2=stack/4 → 掉 3/4（:53356）
  return 0.5;                              // 经典/旅程：num2=stack/2 → 掉 1/2（:53353）
}

export class Clock {
  /** 昼夜边界（1:1 Main.time：昼 4:30AM-7:30PM=54000t、夜 32400t；0=午夜线性 24h） */
  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，随存档持久化——WorldFile.cs:1277-1278 写 / 2070-2072
   *  读，serialize meta.eclipse 同语义）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64899-64920，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721-64725）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段。
   *  触发/清除实现在 world/Eclipse.ts */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;

```

```
  }

/** RandomizeCaveBackgrounds（1456 WorldGen.cs:8157-8210）：按世界宽掷 caveBackX 边界与
 *  caveBackStyle（互异）。尺寸分支计数 2/3/4；掷骰序与 treeX 同族——先全量边界、
 *  后样式（retry 环左→右）。尾部无条件 iceBackStyle/hellBackStyle/jungleBackStyle
 *  3 掷（cs:8211-8213）在调用方（Reset 段）照掷。
 *  区间真值（真 exe 取整，.wld 头实测锚定）：4200:[1050,3150)；
 *  6400:[857,3417)/[2988,5548)；8400:[840,3360)/[2940,5460)/[5040,7560)。 */
export function randomizeCaveBackgrounds(rng: RNG, w: number): { caveBackX: number[]; caveBackStyle: number[] } {
  const caveBackX = [0, 0, 0], caveBackStyle = [0, 0, 0, 0];
  const roll8 = () => rng.int(0, 7);
  if (w === 4200) {
    caveBackX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.25), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.25) - 1);
    caveBackX[1] = w; caveBackX[2] = w;
    caveBackStyle[0] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[1] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[1] === caveBackStyle[0]) caveBackStyle[1] = roll8();
  } else if (w === 6400) {
    caveBackX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.334 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.334 + w * 0.2) - 1);
    caveBackX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.667 - w * 0.2), Math.floor(w * 0.667 + w * 0.2) - 1);
    caveBackX[2] = w;
    caveBackStyle[0] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[1] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[2] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[1] === caveBackStyle[0]) caveBackStyle[1] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[2] === caveBackStyle[0] || caveBackStyle[2] === caveBackStyle[1]) caveBackStyle[2] = roll8();
  } else {
    caveBackX[0] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.25 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.25 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    caveBackX[1] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.5 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.5 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    caveBackX[2] = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.75 - w * 0.15), Math.floor(w * 0.75 + w * 0.15) - 1);
    caveBackStyle[0] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[1] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[2] = roll8(); caveBackStyle[3] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[1] === caveBackStyle[0]) caveBackStyle[1] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[2] === caveBackStyle[0] || caveBackStyle[2] === caveBackStyle[1]) caveBackStyle[2] = roll8();
    while (caveBackStyle[3] === caveBackStyle[0] || caveBackStyle[3] === caveBackStyle[1] || caveBackStyle[3] === caveBackStyle[2]) caveBackStyle[3] = roll8();
  }
  return { caveBackX, caveBackStyle };
}
```

```
### 大世界冒烟

### 重要发现（需主线知晓，oracle 在禁区未动）
**caves-oracle.cs 的中/大 treeX/caveBackX 区间字面量是错的**：`[1076,2136)/[2820,3880)/[840,2520)…` 与真 exe（1456 反编译 cs:8082/8157，且 7 个真实 .wld 值全部落在）`6400:[857,3417)/[2988,5548)`、`8400:[840,3360)/[2940,5460)/[5040,7560)` 矛盾。区间不影响流位（Next 上下界不动流），故其 (surface,rock) 锚点仍有效；但 oracle 侧任何 treeX/caveBackX **值**校验都会偏差。现有 JS `randomizeTreeStyle` 的区间本来就对（只有 fix0 拷贝 bug）。
```
